- **axiomatic-induction**: The project constitution AND the reasoning method. Universally: make illegal states unrepresentable, keep changes surgical, and verify with a real run. - **axiomcode**: Use for any why, what or where question about code — how a codebase works, where something lives, who calls it, what a change to it breaks, which tests cover an edit. Also use when resolving an issue or bug report, which names a symptom rather than a file.
